+## Task4C1 — fix round1/5: executing checkout identity
+
+Review BASE525d992ccdfa24bdd4f6b71c4873a990d900142f. Exact finding: [P2] Bind embedded Node compilation to the verified checkout — scripts/build-finite-native-package.mjs:37. Spec Changes requested, quality1P2/noP1. The exported compiler verified checkout B and its Worker inputs while imported buildMcpNodeProgram() selected Node source beside the executing compiler in checkout A.
+
+Finding and root ruling were committed BEFORE production implementation in bb1a92711b350fed51bc5f60c331d41b16e6a677. Root selected the narrow source-owned restriction: reject a different canonical executing repository root before any build; no generic builder injection, dynamic import or relabeling.
+
+Production fix codeHEAD b2481cdb4dcaef72eb353a0db97938538d7f4aef: resolve requested cwd with realpath, compare against the realpath of the executing compiler repository root derived from import.meta.url, and use the resolved canonical cwd for all subsequent source/configuration/Worker build checks. The existing imported Node builder therefore belongs to the same verified physical checkout. A symlink alias of that checkout is accepted and then discarded in favor of its canonical target; another checkout is refused.
+
+The hosted regression creates an actual temporary Git worktree B, commits a distinct Node server source, supplies a dependency directory symlink solely on the hosted runner, proves B is independently clean/verified with verifyMcpReleaseSource, then calls the real compiler imported from A with cwd=B/revisionB. RED fails with Missing expected rejection, not a fixture/setup error. A separate same-root symlink case remains accepted. Both bounded temporary directories and the worktree are removed after tests; no owner-local source/deps/build was created.

+# AFW — cierre del 2 de noviembre de 2026
+
+Guardado Root: 3nov2026 00:55UTC (2nov21:55Argentina). Gabriel pidió detener el trabajo y apagar el ordenador en diez minutos. No iniciar nuevos bloques, ensayos, OAuth, permisos ni credenciales hasta su regreso. No hay una ocurrencia PC-off preparada; el apagado de hoy no la acredita.
+
+## Avance de hoy
+
+- B2a aceptado como código tras revisión independiente y corrección: respaldo privado saneado, carga de candidata INACTIVA, SEND durable, ACK real y comprobación del UUID/configuración/bytes. Root verificó45pruebas dirigidas/lint; ningún upload real fue ejecutado por este bloque.
+- B2b implementó el dueño del cierre inicial con la alarma DO existente, sin infraestructura nueva. Versión previa b38d pasó35pruebas específicas+163regresiones+23compilador. La revisión detectó3Important: cierre guardado sin validar toda la cadena, activación anterior a ACK de armado y lease duplicado.
+- Fixround1 guardado: funcional4119c5c tuvo RED4fallos→GREEN4; extracción compartida y nuevos controles en2b734f69e3a296ba1043bc2dd372581dc54ae58f/tree08c33ed42d499ef997d5b963ea8a7ed8ca558755. Docscheckpoint0da7df964a2ad8389fdec06b3bd2f5b103f3008f/tree2af811c1616562ed7be305bb22db866d2dc56149. Root leyó metadatos y logs: Actions41555913917/attempt1,45pruebas específicas+lint PASS (job125654628551),23compilerPASS(job125654628446),0fallos/cancel/skip en esos grupos. Job125654628157 de consumidores B1/upload seguía en ejecución al comprobarlo; no afirmar resultado ni repetirlo mañana sin leerlo.
+- Compilación EXACTA2b734f69: canary1948550bytes,QA1939633,residente382788; grafos318/295sinissuer/compiler/fs/esbuild. Artefactos0 a00:55. Alarm delivery manual y activaciones futuras fueron SYN; no es una alarma live ni capacidad9aa demostrada. Same-reviewer scoped review pendiente, FIX_BASEfd6fb939.
+- Se aclaró la procedencia del QA histórico y el antiguo restoreblocked antes de SEND. El dispatch incierto de ese candidato necesita una admisión que no aparece en la historia conservada; validar esquema inmutable e historia primaria en su gate futuro, sin declarar drenaje universal.
+- Cloudflare mostró baseline2607 como desplegable a00:24:02UTC. Se corrigió una incompatibilidad real entre el formato object.items/page0 de deployable=true y el catálogo no filtrado distinto. Ver informe histórico para evidencia y límites.
+
+## Orden de continuación
+
+1. Leer resultado final, logs e inventario del run41555913917 antes de cualquier repetición. Si pasa, generar paquete fd6fb939..fixDocHEAD y pedir revisión scoped al MISMO /root/review_initial_cleanup_owner; fijar cualquier hallazgo sin saltar el gate. Actualizar informe/ledger. No aceptar B2b solo por los tests.
+2. B2c: activación inicial real en MISMO winner, cápsula, source e ingreso autenticado closed_ui. Plazo técnico I0+5min distinto de ventana del usuario de hasta15min desde MISMOI0; cambiar expiry actual5min deliberadamente. No permitir POST de aprobación/business antes de cleanup/handoff aplicable.
+3. Cleanup/handoff para lectura, grants y avisos; forwarding real; montaje nominal/Save; suscripción→entrega útil/revisión→baja y cierre físico. QA finalizer separado. Native9aa/custodia/capacidades siguen gates reales.
+4. CI compuesto, revisión de rama y promoción controlada; recorrido real completo con ordenador encendido y cierre verificado; DESPUÉS una ocurrencia genuina con ordenador apagado. Max sigue sin tocarse y requiere preview/aprobación.
+
+El límite fuente y vencimiento previsto de lector9aa/publicador057 es3nov02:59UTC (2nov23:59Argentina). Mañana comprobar metadata/custodia y resolver autorización necesaria antes de preparar un ensayo; NO renovar, retimar ni reutilizar automáticamente. GHA9db separado conserva su fecha8nov; no es alias del lector nativo.

La calificación que ya está en GitHub puede terminar allí sin la PC; eso no constituye el ensayo AFW PC-off. No nuevos jobs después del STOP. No crear guardia ni cambiar monitores de otros proyectos.

+# C3 finite admission — read-only preflight and race refinement
+
+Source inspected by /root/finite_admission_preflight at ba452cffcf10def0263164a18551372ba15ffd82. No writes, tests, provider/runtime/private probe, callback, secret, grant or effect. Root read systematic-debugging and separately inspected exact custody/host functions. This is evidence/contract proposal, not implementation or runtime finding.
+
+## Exact causal order
+
+- mcp-own-durable-host.mjs57–68: subscription snapshot/authorization/closed check → receipts.preflight → invocation → custody.start → controller.subscribe(callback verification) → receipts.record → readReadiness.
+- mcp-node-process-custody.mjs79–90: finite.beginAdmission marker precedes nativeD1 admission.93–100 D1 admission→encrypted custody reserved→starting.103 launch checks reservation too late.
+- Custody45–55 admissionSQL checks originalgrant/latestconsent/absenceclosure/revoke/scope/time/window/deadline, NOT preparedreservation.
+- mcp-successor-launch-gate29–35: optional launchmarker then authorization/deployment/retirement and launchclaimINSERT joins exact prepared budget1 reservation.
+- mcp-successor-reservation26–35: reservationINSERT requires matching originalpredecessor admission/closure and GLOBAL admissionsCOUNT=1; premature secondadmission poisons intendedreservation.
+- Host70–76 catch alwayscustody.close; custody144–159 LEFTJOIN explicitlyallows closure withnoadmission andrequests runtime.close. Early guard onlyinside custody.start stillcausesclosure; pretryguard alone stillfailsrace if laterprecheck/INSERT eligibilitychanges.
+- worker/mcp-own/index30–40 doesn'tpassfiniteGate tohost; actualAfwNodeControl89–95 sameDOpath. Library-onlyfix doesnotestablishactualfinitewiring.
+- mcp-own-finite-cleanup-host13–18 derives actualsourcegate fromvalidatedcontrol/ctx/storage/DB/namespace/version/primary/nominal; resolverstillfactorygated.
+- mcp-own-finite-primary12–22 permitsnullreservation andhashesonread; omitscopeHash/predecessormatch.24–28settlementrowpresence isnotadmissionpermit.
+- mcp-subscription-lifecycle153–173 stillreadinessbeforecallbackverification;150cloudReviewConfirmedfalse. SeparateC3 gate.
+
+## Minimal proposed reservation checks (Root must rule before product)
+
+Dedicated source-owned/module-branded finite admissionprecondition, noarguments/callerDTO/boolean/hashpermit. Derived fromactualfixedvalidatedcontrol+resolvedsuccessor. Checkexactslot/trial/scopehash/candidatecontainer/program/source/window/application/predecessorpins, prepared/budget1 state andoriginalpredecessor admission/closure, currentcontrol/source/primaryclock.
+
+Host invokesprecondition BEFOREcleanup-on-failure try; custody.start repeatsBEFOREbeginAdmission foralternatefixedcompositions. ActualD1 admissionINSERT mustALSOjoin/EXISTS sameexactfixedreservationpredicate tocloseTOCTOU. Keepalloldgrant/consent/closure/scope/SQLtimechecks. NativeDOmarker andD1 aredifferentstores: no atomicityclaim/no markerreset/refund afterissued.
+
+Historicalreservation.observed_at isnotnew30slifetime; currentlaunch'sfreshretirementobservationstaysseparate. Preserve retainFiniteWriterDeadline(gate117–123), double nominalchecks47–54/70, seals/N1/C1/originaldeadlines/unknownACK. No genericnewserver/routes/permissions.
+
+## Trusted classification needed toavoidclosing a rejected attempt
+
+PrivateWeakSet of provedno-admission errors scoped custodyinstance/currentstart; onlycustodyimplementationcanmint. Internalreader e.g.isKnownNoAdmissionFailure(error) recognizesonlyexistingbrand, notflags/DTOs. Custody.safe narrowlypreservesthatexacterrorobject, sanitizeseveryothererror; hostcatchskipsclosureONLYitsowncustodyrecognizesthebrand. Outerhost.safeALWAYSgenericpublicfailure. No nativebrand/code/state overRPC/HTTP.
+
+Mintonly:
+1 finitepreconditiondenial BEFOREbeginAdmission/admission/runtime, undertrustedfreshattemptstate. Existingunresolvedadmission/launchwriter/ambiguouspriorstate NOTsafe.
+2 alladmissionexecutions inthisstart ACKed success===true ANDmeta.changes===0; nonepending/unknown, no runtimelaunch. Issuedfinite markerstaysspent/unresolved. Noretrypermission.
+
+Actualclaimuses ordinaryINSERT…SELECT .run(), NOTORIGNORE/RETURNING/.first. Resolvedsuccess:true+changes0 proves THAT executionzerowrites; currentchanges!==1erroralone doesnot. Existingrowuniquenessconflictcanthrow: notzero. Exception/timeout/malformedresult/successfalse/changes1/postINSERTfail/lostACK/custodypersistfail/readinessfail/possibleruntime retainsordinarycleanup. Do notskipcleanup basedonpublicerrorname/code.
+
+D1executionisinsidenativeDO.storage.transaction callback93–96. Trackallattempt evidenceOUTSIDEcallback; callbackrerunmustnotforgetpriorchanges1/unknown. Anyinserted/unknown permanentlydisqualifiesno-admissionbrandforthisstart. OnlyallACKzero/nopending/nolaunchqualifies.
+
+## Focused future RED/GREEN
+
+1 ActualnativeDO/D1 premature subscribe todayproducessecondadmissionorclosure andblocks subsequentreservation. GREENleavescompleteDO/D1snapshotunchanged,0launch/close/callback; intendedreservationstillinsertable.
+2 Eachwrongfixedpin/missingrow/notyet/expired/control/consentrevocation/readfailure deniesbeforemarkers.
+3 Hostprecheckpasses/custodyprecheckfails: no closure/close.
+4 Bothprechecks pass/eligibilityexpires/actualINSERTACKzero: noadmission/closure/close/launch, DOmarker unresolved(no refund).
+5 Throw/lostACK/malformed/successfalse: cleanupretained.
+6 INSERTsuccess/laterfailure: cleanupretained.

+10 Actualfinitehostwiring/factoryqualificationisowninggate; don'tclaimfromlibraryfixture. Callback-readiness/Save/earlywithdraw/receiver/fullpackage remainseparate.

@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+# C3 premature finite admission — refinement for implementation
+
+Source-only ruling; no runtime repair, new grant, owner window, native launch or provider action. Extends reports/2026-11-02-finite-admission-preflight.md. The source race is established there; no speculative credential/provider attribution.
+
+## Outcome and boundary
+
+A premature finite subscribe must not insert the second admission or closure and poison the later prepared reservation. A launch guard alone is too late. A JavaScript early check alone leaves a D1 race. Preserve cleanup for every unknown admission result or possible process. Existing ordinary/nonfinite behavior, grant/consent/SQL time fences, original finite writer lease and settlement/reduction semantics stay intact.
+
+Only bounded library composition is selected initially; actual Worker finiteGate wiring remains a separately qualified owning gate. Callback readiness, Save, useful receiver turn, early withdrawal and full publication package are separate.
+
+## Native source, lazy admission, cleanup independence
+
+createFiniteNativePrimaryReader accepts optional all-or-none nativeAdmission:{ctx,env,program,programSourceRevision}. Capture and validate actual fixed bindings, control identity, program/source and DB equivalence; no request facts/callbacks/SQL/clock overrides beyond the existing internal constructor. Return a private source-specific admissionSource with provenance tied to this DB/control/candidate, never a caller-enrolled callback-shaped object or generic registration.
+
+Existing read/reservation/admission/launch settlement readers remain available without admission authority. New admissionSource is not a ready permit. IMPORTANT: resolve readOwnSuccessorHost/resolveSuccessorPreparation/current LAZILY on admission use. Constructors also serve cleanup after Save/revocation/N1; requiring a live grant/window there would break reductions, sealing, physical retirement and historical observations. No recursion through cleanup factory to resolve an admission source. Exact resolver dependencies and closed behavior must be verified before code.
+
+Existing createFiniteAdmissionGate exact options gain admissionSource. Its private record holds the source helper. Internal assertFinitePreparedAdmission(gate) and finiteAdmissionInsertConstraint(gate,internallyCreatedManifest,originalIssuedLease) use that existing brand; neither accepts SQL, reservation DTO, ready flag or arbitrary callback.
+
+Native cleanup factory supplies actual ctx/env/nativeCleanup program and revision into this source, then the gate. Source validates the actual fixed successor policy, resolved candidate trial/container/program/source/window and original application against finite control. Preserve current-holder/source checks around each admission query and before generating SQL constraints. Settlement hashes never grant admission.
+
+## Fixed prepared-reservation D1 constraint
+
+Use source-owned literal predicates/parameter order, not caller SQL. The existing admission INSERT...SELECT must additionally EXISTS the actual prepared successor reservation joined to its matching original predecessor admission and closure. Preserve all original admission grant/owner/consent/revocation/closure/scope/time predicates.
+
+The reservation joins original admission by predecessor container/generation/program/source/image cloudflare/debian-trixie and closure by actual grant/container/generation/program/source/image. Predicate requires slot afw.own-successor.v1, resolved trial/scopeHash/original application, predecessor pins, candidate container/program/source, exact start/expiry, matching closed_at, state prepared, budget1, T0<=reserved_at<=SQL_NOW and start<=SQL_NOW<expiry. The generation comes from the actual joins, not a fabricated parameter. No global admissionsCOUNT=1 here; that is reservation creation's separate condition.
+
+Read additionally clips SQL_NOW to min(N1,candidate.expiry,selectors.expiry). INSERT adds manifest.expiry<=reservation.expiry and SQL_NOW<min(manifest.expiry,original issued lease.deadline), with reservation aliases INSIDE EXISTS. Use actual D1 millisecond expression, not JavaScript Date.now. A historical reservation observed_at never creates a renewed 30-second lifetime. Existing launch retains fresh retirement/deployment checks.
+
+Only successful cardinality-checked first-primary query can establish missing/not-eligible reservation as definite denial. Read error/malformed result is ambiguous, never a ready or safe-zero permit.
+
+## Cleanup routing: trusted private classification
+
+One per-custody/current-start private error brand, minted only inside that implementation. Internal isKnownNoAdmissionFailure(error) recognizes only that invocation's existing private object; never public error text/code/boolean/DTO. Custody safe wrapper preserves ONLY this exact object, sanitizes others; host catch skips custody.close ONLY on recognition by its own custody. Outer host safe always emits existing generic failure.
+
+Precondition guards belong INSIDE routing that applies this classification, before beginAdmission/admission/runtime; not a generic pre-try throw that accidentally avoids cleanup. Repeat at custody boundary for alternative trusted compositions.
+
+Brand only:
+1. Source-owned fresh-attempt observations establish no prior unresolved admission/launch/custody/finite writer, then definite precondition denial occurs before beginAdmission/admission/runtime.
+2. Every actual D1 execution in this start resolved success===true and meta.changes===0, none pending/unknown, no possible launch.
+
+The finite marker, if issued, remains spent/unresolved; no reset/refund/retry permission. Actual ordinary INSERT...SELECT .run() is retained, not ORIGNORE/RETURNING/.first. Thrown uniqueness error is NOT acknowledged zero. success:false/missing or malformed metadata/changes1/lost ACK/post-insert authorization or custody persistence/readiness failure/possible runtime all retain cleanup. Later read showing no row is not proof of zero writes.
+
+D1 execution lives inside a retriable DO.storage.transaction callback. Cumulative pending/unknown/inserted evidence must live OUTSIDE callback. A later rerun with changes0 cannot erase any earlier changes1, unknown or pending operation. Keep underlying operation retention and original clocks, no new admission lifetime.
+
+## Focused qualification
+
+Use actual workerd SQLite DO and D1, actual host/custody/gate/primary/reservation composition, explicit SYN source/custody/approval/runtime boundaries. Meaningful RED must demonstrate the current premature-admission/closure race and atomic SQL gap, not a missing import alone.
+
+- Missing reservation premature subscribe leaves complete DO/D1 snapshots unchanged, zero launch/close/callback, and intended reservation later remains insertable.
+- Wrong exact source/control/predecessor/scope/trial/application/candidate/window/budget/state and not-yet/expired reservation deny before markers.
+- Host guard passes then custody guard denies: trusted fresh case skips close, ambiguous prior state retains cleanup.
+- Both pass but reservation/consent/time changes before INSERT: ACK success/changes0 skips close, marker remains unresolved.
+- Throw/lost ACK/malformed/successfalse or changes1/later failure retain cleanup.
+- Callback reruns changes1/unknown then0 still retain cleanup; pending underlying promise never treated as settled.
+- Existing unresolved admission/launch/custody cannot mint safe brand; forged public Error/DTO cannot suppress close.
+- Valid reservation admits at most once under original lease; existing launch fences unchanged.
+- Construction and historical read/seal/close/reduction remain possible after Save/revoke/N1, while admission denies.
+- Ordinary nonfinite paths unchanged; actual Worker mount still unavailable unless explicitly separately qualified.
+
+Source acceptance never establishes active grant, physical process settlement, subscription, useful cloud review, native unsubscribe or PC-off. operationalReadiness=false; completePackage=false; PCoff=false.
+
+
+## Confirmed lazy call-path delta (read-only followup)
+
+At44dde8d: cleanup-host18,23–40 constructs primary/gate before exposing settlement, seal, withdrawal, close and retirement. finite-primary10–26 has historical reads without live-grant/N1 cutoff. finite-admission-gate45,58–70,74–80 separates begin/N1 from settle/seal/C1. successor-host17–27 validates enabled policy/current bindings and creates provider adapters; these must be resolved only on admission use. worker/mcp-own/index23–28,30–34,73–81 deliberately uses deferred close and historical bypass.
+
+Minimum private source methods assertPrepared() and insertConstraint(manifest,originalLease), fresh on each invocation. They must not call #finite, #host, cleanup factory/resolver or nativeCleanup.close. No successful readiness cache. Existing read/settle/seal must never invoke this helper. This explicitly supersedes the read-only agent's earlier eager-constructor proposal.
+
+## Root implementation clarifications — preserved chronology
+
+Before product, Root explicitly approved the reservation-only distinction, recorded in the pre-code plan at6af54224896e9d6574bd10f39d374a997badb024. This supersedes the ambiguous blanket "prior finite writer" wording above: a valid issued/pending reservation writer cannot itself create custody/process and does not disqualify fresh pre-admission rejection. Any admission/launch writer, corresponding primary admission/launch/closure, custody record or unreadable/malformed/ambiguous prior state still disqualifies. No marker is reset/refunded. Purpose: avoid poisoning legitimate in-progress reservation completion; risk if wrong: missed possible-process cleanup. Actual producer ownership and native regression qualify the bounded distinction.
+
+During qualification, Root confirmed absent native source must deny admission SQL/runtime.launch through BOTH changed host/custody guards. Standalone createSuccessorLaunchGate remains separately owned and unchanged, including its existing lease tests. Journal begin* alone is not effect permission. This unit does not establish standalone launch authority, actual Worker wiring or factory qualification.
+
+Self-review then reproduced a cumulative-state edge atc943a005: ACK0 followed by malformed custody in a retried callback incorrectly suppressed close. Root confirmed before the correction that any ambiguous prior state is sticky, even after zero, and must retain generic cleanup. Final06e424 recognizes only the exact privately tracked zero-execution failure with all execution evidence safe; every other callback error/prior custody is sticky unsafe. Final run41537047289/job125591439327/attempt1:65 affected +23 compiler/residency PASS,0fail/cancel/skip,lint clean,artifacts0. Full source-only handoff: [report](2026-11-02-finite-premature-admission.md). Independent review remains pending.

+## Recommended boundary
+
+Narrowcompiledfinitecomposition sharingactualSDK/read/events/domainprimitives + source-ownedfixedcapabilities. SmallestnextsourceafterRoot/T5: existinggrantread + authenticatedrevokeonlyHTTP throughactualSDK→handler→D1 tests. SeparatefollowonfiniteGate/Save/capture/delivery/physicalsettlement. +## Concrete timing dependencies
+
+- lib/mcp-own-durable-host.mjs:57–68 — subscribe→receipts.preflight→custody.start→controller.subscribe(callbackverify)→receiptpersist→readiness. mcp-subscription-lifecycle155,169–173 checksnodeReadiness beforecallbackverification. No split callback-only preauthorization mode.
+- lib/mcp-node-process-custody.mjs:79–90,93–103 — beginAdmission beforeactualadmissionINSERT; actualadmissionwritten BEFORE successorlaunch discoversmissingreservation.
+- lib/mcp-own-finite-admission-gate.mjs + finite-trial-control.mjs:47–54 /296–306 — Admission requires actualarmedcontrol/withdrawnAtnull/nominalclaim/currentnotice. prepare→guardarm→claimEffectnominal actuallaunch88–101. No preHEADcallback success viafiniteGate.
+- lib/mcp-release-runtime.mjs + successor-reservation.mjs + successor-launch-gate.mjs:77–82,121–138 /29–36 /29–42 — Nominal150–160s→runReleaseOperator→activationconfirm→reserveReleaseSuccessor writespreparedbudget1 reservation. Launchjoinsreservationprepared/budget1. PrematuresubscribeafterARM butbeforenominalreserve canINSERTadmissionthenfail; reservationexpectsglobaladmissioncount1, so it canblocklaterreserve. C3 requires rejection BEFOREmarkers/D1write ifreservationnotproven.
+- lib/delegated-dossier-events.mjs + dossier-monitoring-authority.mjs + permission-withdrawal.mjs:37–42 /72–78 /145–176 — Actualclientunsubscribe stillrequiresOAuthactive g.revoked_at=='' andunexpired; noticecatalognotrequired. Serverunsubscribe→custody.close atdurablehost109. C0withdrawnotices thenreadcouldpreventlaterclientunsubscribe. Servercleanup/stoprequest≠clientnativeunsubscribe orphysicalstop.
+
+Actual sequence beforePCoff: ARMclaim→nominal+reservation→subscribe/Container/callback→receipt/readiness→poweroff→Save/event. No provenbarrier delayingSave/eventuntilcallbackanduseroff, no guaranteedlatencies, nofirstHEADPConreplay. Root must inspect actualKnown18stage ordering for existingreadiness barrier before changinganyclock/stage. Timing/artifact/context sourceplan assumptions need explicitRootruling; no clockchange approved.
+
+Root inspected actual mcp-release-runtime.mjs atBASE: runKnownCancellationReleaseWorkflow preserves fourargs/18hooks/70s and confirms activation/reservation; no Saveproducer appears in that inspected path. Finite CLI admission/seed-material/admit/poll/restore likewise does not itself trigger Save. A source-authorized Save producer and client-ready/PC-off timing relation must be designed explicitly; do not assume fixed Save timing, add a callback, shift clocks or mutate Known by inference. Read grant never authorizes Save; reader plugin promises noediting. Prefer separate preapproved operator-domain action, not a hidden write tool in read-only plugin. These are cross-task design gates, not effects performed.
+
+## Official consumer route (Root verification)
+
+Root searched and actually opened [OpenAI MCP Events](https://developers.openai.com/plugins/build/mcp-events) on2Nov2026. It documents Workweb, WorkdesktopCloud and dots; ChatGPT supplies callbackURL/signingsecret during events/subscribe. Its test path requires actual subscription, callbackverification, delivery, useful response and unsubscribe. This establishes a supported client route, not actual AFW account/chat/AFWOperations environment binding or PCoff. Absence of a tool in current session does not prove platform unsupported. No callback/key/chat/subscription/automation created by this research.
+
+## Required fixes / later gates
+
+Premature subscribe needs rejection BEFORE newadmission/markers if exactpreparedreservation absent; don't use it as harmless probe. Client-native unsubscribe must occur while OAuth/readgrant stillvalid; noticewithdrawal alone permits conceptualunsubscribe but readrevocation blocks it. Serverteardown and Containerclose request are separate from clientunsubscribe and physicalstop. Current source doesn'tprove a safe shutdown interval; preserve original150/160,C0/C1,N1/R1/firstHEAD and require a concrete sequence before an actualtrial. +# AFW: alcance de los escritores históricos del 30 de octubre
+
+Revisión Root del 2 de noviembre de 2026. Solo lectura; no se repitió el ensayo ni se cambiaron recursos. Esta nota corrige dos incertidumbres concretas del preflight anterior, sin declarar drenaje universal ni estado operativo actual.
+
+## Procedencia del código realmente conservado
+
+Dos GET acotados al Worker QA agent-friendly-web-independent-closure-qa, cuenta85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec, identificaron la versión histórica01ac6aa0-3ec8-43a7-bf19-31e46cad82d9, creada30oct16:38:54.653139UTC. El segundo calculó dentro del ejecutor Cloudflare, sin devolver código ni bindings, index.js462861bytes/SHA256a7d40b811f8817ffeb895f0b95ff7c83f8bea987b17987f8d4640f7b7e5916f7. La metadata antigua exports[] no describe necesariamente los exports del código.
+
+Root consultó el run41083490837 y el log primario del job124158457457: main/push/attempt1/SUCCESS30oct15:44:32–15:45:04UTC. El log imprime build.json con purpose afw.independent-closure-build.v1, sourceRevision2a4773737764c5038aaa0b3528b43df2a0572441, sourceTree637af253296ac1c771cb73ea353cb960a2e59204, configurationHash99717696167662708e236bd3896368d5489620bfc930c62b98b03e97505b0d52 y el MISMO módulo index.js462861bytes/hasha7d40b81 completo anterior. remotePublished=false y operationalReadiness=false son campos del manifiesto de compilación.
+
+La receta .github/workflows/afw-independent-closure-build.yml@2a477 L48–89 usa Node22/npmci/Wranglerdryrun, verifica revisión e inputs y escribe/imprime el mismo manifiesto. El recibo docs/AFW-RETIREMENT-RECEIPT-CLOSED-RELEASE-2026-10-30.es.md L17 vincula esos bytes y build a QA78af posterior. El módulo01ac coincide por hash/bytes primarios, no por el nombre de una revisión. No se extrajo ZIP ni se reconstruyó localmente. El digest ZIP00c0fdaa… es distinto del digest de módulo y no se sustituyó.
+
+Esto permite inspeccionar el código exacto2a477 del módulo correlacionado. No es una atestación criptográfica de ejecución por cada fila histórica, ni convierte al lectorM actual en productor de esa historia.
+
+## Reducción COMPLETE y destroy concreto
+
+En source2a477:
+- worker/mcp-own/index.mjs L56–60 lleva withdrawPermissionsForPlan al cierre protegido del host.
+- lib/mcp-own-permission-withdrawal-host.mjs L39–76 verifica programa/fuente, grant/container/policy originales y recorta el plazo absoluto recibido.
+- lib/mcp-own-durable-host.mjs L43–46,109 entrega el runtime original real.
+- lib/mcp-original-container-runtime.mjs L64 contiene await container.destroy(). No existe en esta fuente una rama de éxito por simplemente no encontrar instancia.
+- lib/mcp-node-process-custody.mjs L119–141 espera ese cierre antes de devolver closed:true/runtimeStopRequested:true.
+- lib/mcp-own-permission-withdrawal.mjs L117–128,163–169 solo conserva runtimeStopRequested:true tras el cierre exitoso; error o timeout deja false.
+- lib/mcp-hosted-canary-restoration.mjs L191–201 exige ese true antes de escribir reduction COMPLETE.
+
+Conclusión acotada: en ESA invocación que escribió un nuevo COMPLETE, el Promise nativo concreto de destroy ya había cumplido. El campo genérico physicalStopVerified:false no anula ese await concreto; tampoco permite extrapolar cierre de todos los procesos o solicitudes.
+
+## Primaria del guard y ausencia de SEND de restauración en ESA invocación
+
+PROJECT AFW; REPOSITORY tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web; ENVIRONMENT QA histórico cerrado; ORIGIN api.cloudflare.com/client/v4; RESOURCE_TYPE Workflow instance status; RESOURCE_ID afw-own-qa-private-bootstrap/afw-mcp-closure-d56b5e850d24cc23058a024d73bae4c830a91c1fb9659f2e96282854ccae2c04; ALLOWED_ACTION un GET, salida saneada por lista blanca; ROLLBACK no aplica, sin mutación.
+
+GET primario del2nov2026 a23:51:33.482UTC:
+- status complete; start30oct16:39:05.020UTC; end16:41:01.728UTC; step_count6.
+- output: status blocked, dispatchStatus uncertain, nativeWithdrawalVerified false, operationalReadiness false, mayRetry false.
+- reduce-own-original-permissions-1: un intento success,16:41:00.050–16:41:01.541UTC.
+- restore-own-closed-canary-1: un intento success,16:41:01.564–16:41:01.675UTC.
+
+El comentario primario497#6528223100,30oct16:46:36UTC, correlaciona QA01ac/guardd56, reducción, restoreblocked, retiro del control16:42:07.958 y restauración administrativa canary2607/deployment7b5a posterior. Su physicalStopfalse y dispatchuncertain se preservan; no se reescribe el recibo histórico.
+
+Root y una revisión independiente comprobaron la implicación en source2a477:
+- hosted Workflow L300–305 añade dispatchStatus al devolver el resultado del restorer; su outercatch después de touched devolvería uncertain, no este blocked con dispatchStatus.
+- El host puede bloquear antes de construir transporte, por ejemplo al fallar la captura de handoff L218–224.
+- mcp-successor-canary-restorer.mjs L154–175 fija issued=true ANTES del put del journal y antes del único POST /workers/scripts/agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary/deployments. Nunca vuelve a false. Un fallo posterior devuelve uncertain, no blocked.
+- Si vence antes de issuance, finally fija finished=true/abort; checks de continuación/call L104–119,174–176 impiden enviar después.
+
+Conclusión: bajo la correlación fuente/ejecución establecida, el resultado primario blocked de ese único intento cierra la incertidumbre de restore-SEND de ESA invocación. No se justifica seguir tratándola como un POST de restauración pendiente. No es necesario otro ensayo para repetir esta conclusión.
+
+## Lo que permanece separado
+
+El prepared dispatch anterior sigue uncertain; la reducción exitosa no drena una solicitud anterior deliverOwnSaved. Ese código escribe el evento/grant/historia antiguos; no publica la versión nueva ni se transforma en nueva autoridad. Deben seguir verificándose el aislamiento de escrituras antiguas, sus selectores y la reutilización del Container compartido al integrar el nuevo flujo. Otros guards, solicitudes administrativas o escritores no quedan descartados por este único resultado.
+
+Para una futura activación hacen falta inventarios actuales, exacta procedencia y SEND relevantes, control de puntero propio y rollback, y capacidad de los endpoints realmente usada por la clave nativa custodiada. La consulta fue por la sesión MCP Cloudflare del owner: no acredita esa capacidad del lector9aa. El reader-probe existente consulta siete recursos con la clave cargada, pero no verifica id del token ni GET individual de Workflow; no se ejecutó.
+
+B2a inactive deploy=false no requiere el drenaje de activación: un restorer antiguo selecciona una versión vieja y no activa/elimina el UUID nuevo. Su version-catalog/latest/last100 sí puede variar; cada consumidor debe fijar UUID explícito. La composición completa, la activación, la entrega útil, la baja y PC-off siguen pendientes.
+
+operationalReadiness=false; completePackage=false; PCoff=false.
+
+## Narrower candidate-specific dispatch conclusion (3 November source follow-up)
+
+The generic admitted-and-ready deliverOwnSaved path can call native setInactivityTimeout and Container port fetch during runtime.observe; it is therefore incorrect to describe every such pending dispatch as database-only. It cannot itself call start/replacement; start is the separate subscription/custody.start path.
+
+For THIS old guard, source2a477 hosted-restoration183–186 explicitly targets p.candidate.containerId, despite the local variable named original. successor-manifest16–17 derives the successor ID distinct from manifest.original.containerId. Host99–103 requires the exact same candidate/grant admission+receipt eligibility row BEFORE readReadiness; SQL in mcp-own-subscription-receipt11–21 joins container/process generation/program/source and rejects revoked/closed/expired. The original different container's admission cannot match it. With preserved append-only candidate-zero admission history, this path cannot reach native observation/inactivity/port/callback at all, including a delayed empty query continuation.
+
+This is a resource-relevance proof conditional on preserved mapping/candidate-zero history and no deletion/rewrite, not a claim the request settled. Revalidate those facts/closures/revocations in the actual native activation/Container-reuse gate; processGeneration:null or current absence alone is insufficient. No new D1 query was made for this source follow-up. This narrows the earlier generic inactivity caveat without erasing dispatchuncertain.
+
+Schema source evidence: db/dossier-node-process.sql@2a477 defines BEFORE UPDATE and BEFORE DELETE triggers that abort for admissions and closures. The inspected custody implementation uses plain INSERT for admission and preserves it through closure. Actual deployed schema equality is not established by a source file; later native gates must check the installed triggers/primary snapshots. Cloudflare's [Container API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/containers/api/durable-object-container/) documents destroy completion and inactivity timeouts per Durable Object; it does not establish settlement/generation binding of arbitrary old operations. The candidate-specific admission barrier avoids claiming that generic isolation fact.
+
+## Dated rollback catalog metadata (3 November)
+
+Root made two distinct read-only metadata GETs on the fixed canary, with no code/bindings/secrets returned. At00:22:32.387UTC, /workers/workers/{worker}/versions?page=1&per_page=100 returned an unfiltered ARRAY,100of171, baseline2607 atindex5/number166. At00:24:02.713UTC, /workers/scripts/{worker}/versions?deployable=true&page=1&per_page=100 returned OBJECT{items:[100]}, result_info{page:0,per_page:100,count:100,total_count:100}, the same baselineatindex5. The [official API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/api/resources/workers/subresources/scripts/subresources/versions/methods/list/) states deployable filtering ignores pagination. These endpoints are not interchangeable; unfiltered membership is not a substitute for the deployable filter. B2b fixture and production validation were corrected against that exact shape/page0, with causal RED→GREEN recorded in its report. This confirms dated metadata membership only, not a deployment, future catalog stability, native9aa capability or current closure of every runtime. No repeat query or effect was performed.

+
+## Post-implementation Root ruling on the clock distinction
+
+This is a disclosed refinement after implementation observation, not a claim that the original pre-code sentence already described the final clock split.
+
+Workflow and actor share the unchanged absolute `baseDeadline = min(native createdAt + 30000, fixed ceiling)`. The actor independently narrows to original owner-command `I0 + 300000` and verified custody expiry before Entry/A, and owns every A/possible future effect continuation. The deterministic record keeps `operationDeadline = min(I0 + 300000, createdAt + 30000, fixed ceiling)`; expiry narrows the invocation only.
+
+The exact safe RPC output carries no deadline. The wrapper can receive factual data after the actor's tighter I0/expiry bound, provided its unchanged native base is still current. Root explicitly approved this distinction because the returned data confers no authority, effect permission or resumable continuation. The cost is potentially delayed factual output; this unit cannot grant a timely effect permit. The original base may never be widened. Future publication must remain inside the surviving winning actor continuation with independent gates. Fresh reviewers must evaluate this amended contract.
+
+## Causal and durability limits
+
+The transaction callback uses only storage and local validation, and its final successful return is the only winner oracle. A retried callback cannot leak a previous outer inserted flag. Native sync is required before exact readback/A and again after A. No allowUnconfirmed is used. A cached get or transaction Promise alone is not substituted for disk persistence.
+
+If an entry never committed, another contender in the same original intent/deadline may attempt the same deterministic pre-effect CAS. If it committed but its ACK was lost, its durable entry blocks every contender. Existing, conflicting, expired, restarted or losing invocations never reconstruct A admission. A crash after durable entry deliberately sacrifices liveness. Unknown A outcome leaves Entry spent.
+
+This exception applies only to the private deterministic pre-effect claim. Unknown actual upload/deploy/Workflow CREATE SEND/ACK never permits a resend. Promise retention, timeout, abort or RPC disposal does not prove physical process settlement or remote cancellation. The accepted factual A was not characterized as unsafe; the new composition adds explicit durability barriers.
+
+Cloudflare primary storage sources are recorded with blobs/line references in the preflight: SQLite async KV includes transaction/sync; put/get can use a buffer; sync resolves after prior writes persist; native transactions are atomic/isolated and callbacks may retry. [SQLite storage API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/api/sqlite-storage-api/) and [Cloudflare transaction explanation](https://blog.cloudflare.com/durable-objects-easy-fast-correct-choose-three/) support the argument.
+

+# Finite native RPC boundary preflight — 2 November 2026
+
+Readonly integration inspection by /root/review_initial_native_entry at ae7afdb8820b509f761ac026d311f9c39ed8b0db after accepted B1. No writes, tests, provider calls or C3 review. Existing native fixtures inspected, not rerun or represented as fresh passing evidence.
+
+## Result
+
+No demonstrable disposer/parser mismatch in the concrete fixed-RPC consumers inspected. Cloudflare system-adds a disposer to object RPC results. Client Symbol.dispose is synchronous and does NOT prove server settlement: https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/#disposing-rpc-objects-disposes-stubs-that-are-part-of-that-object .
+
+| Boundary | Actual parser | Existing qualification / limitation |
+|---|---|---|
+| Gate beginFiniteTrialReservation/settleFiniteTrialReservation, admission-gate107–110 | Raw envelope returned without disposal. successor-reservation31,35 reads status; only nested claim reaches strict exact at gate119. No demonstrated top-level symbol rejection. | Gate tests local; cleanup-native30–44,56 uses fixture→Response.json, not this client. |
+| observeRetiredReceipt, release-successor-reservation39 | Local value box→readRetiredRpcReceipt descriptor normalizer accepts ONLY documented disposer and disposes finally; retired-rpc-receipt12–28 / retired-rpc-observation8–31. | Actual Worker→DO fixture and native lifecycle/invalid-result disposal inspected at retired-rpc-receipt-native32–36,48–74. |
+| prepare/readFiniteNominalLaunch/createFiniteNominalChild, finite-launcher26,29,44 | metadata8 structuredClone then dispose before strict fields/record/data. Compatible factual DTO boundary. | finite-launcher-native12–41,49–64 exercises native RPC/class/binding; source facts/scheduling controls SYN. Do not infer live engine delivery from this inspection. |
+| previewFiniteTrialMaterial, finite-observer37 | metadata12 clone/dispose→validatePreview/finiteData/finiteFields19. Compatible. | finite-observer-native32–34,70–79 native RPC fixture, preview producer explicitly SYN. |
+| CleanupProofHead/RetainedCleanupProof/CleanupCompletion/claimQaRestore/FinalPrimary, observer47–59 | Every result metadata-normalized before status/claim/data checks. Compatible. | Same observer fixture, actual claim journal/D1, external source facts SYN. |
+| setupFinitePrivatePolicy, policy-setup135–144 | Actor result cloned/disposed140 before fields/codec144. Compatible. | finite-policy-setup-worker57,69–76 actual Worker→DO; event/step/source qualification SYN. |
+
+## Owning integration limits
+
+Reservation client's undisposed raw envelope is lifecycle maintenance, not a demonstrated parser failure/capability leak. Defer to integration/final review; don't introduce a permissive shared parser or claim physical drain.
+
+cleanup-collector12–16 strictly fields() nativeProof.ready()/observe() callback results without normalization, but production fixed-RPC mount was NOT established. Future fixed adapter forwarding raw readFiniteTrialCleanupReadiness/observeFiniteTrialCleanup must handle the native boundary; current JSON test bypasses this collector. This is an owning future adapter test requirement, not a currently mounted transport defect.
+
+Confirmation/launch/adoption/source-authority seams inspected remain unavailable constructors, not demonstrated RPC consumers. Their future wiring is unqualified. No additional product fix or trial is justified solely by this read-only inspection.
+
+operationalReadiness=false; completePackage=false; PCoff=false.

+## What exists
+
+The dedicated command is /afw-qa-finite-bootstrap v1 <M>, owner-created issue497/main/attempt1 only, separate from post-T0 finite confirmation and first HEAD. P0 is original comment time, Pend=P0+300000 includes queue/install/compiler/mutations/readbacks and eventual successful step/job. Native can read provenance later if original completed-success timing and fresh current source/QA/custody/parent qualify; no owner15min/read10min clock starts.

+Seven separate lib/mcp-qa-finite-bootstrap-* modules implement exact public grammar/hash, original admission, GitHub primary qualification, narrow QA publication byte/profile read, QA-only literal source commitments, GHA source issuer and unmounted native reader. The actual CLI and future afw-finite-qa-bootstrap.yml use the existing environment and source-scoped credentials. No production-source workflow was dispatched.

QA profile is exact: every required configuration field must exist; extra or unexpected normalization is denied. It calls no full-package qualifier/bypass. CF budget4 GET; GitHub issuer budget32 requests, current success path27. Transport remains bounded and uses original admission.
+
+The source issuer creates only an annotated Git object and its fullUUID ref, with cap spent before first POST. Unknown tag/ref ACK and conflicts never refund or resend. It sees only in-progress original step/job; native later verifies actual completed-success. The public canonical record is <=16KiB and contains only listed technical fields/hashes.
+
+Native reads exactly3 CF (token verify and before/after deployments) +11 anonymous GitHub requests. It checks actual DO namespace/id/storage and env holders, original <=30s parent and <=5s children, retained actual promises, current main/ref, immutable tag object, source-file blob/body hashes, and original completed-success job/step times. No native QA version/content GET, GHA credential, cache/proxy/retry or source-context authority. Direct qaCodeProvenanceVerified=false; scope/source/effects/readiness/fullpackage remain false.

+## Essential trust limitation
+
+Git refs are MUTABLE by other repository writers/admins. This unit implements source-owned write-once/no-reset behavior under the explicit TRUSTED REPOSITORY WRITER/GOVERNANCE ASSUMPTION. Current ref/object/payload/source/job correlation does not prove historical nonmutation, detect an away/back move, establish full actual publisher identity, or grant owner/business permission. Evidence explicitly carries repositoryWriterTrustAssumed:true and historicalRefNonmutationVerified:false. A later initial publisher/owning source wrapper MUST explicitly accept this boundary or require stronger proof before an owner window. No ruleset, key, OIDC or attestation service was inferred.
+
+The named bridge returns qaPublicationBridgeVerified:true only for the bounded source-governed technical facts. It is not a registry, caller context mint or public capability. The source-only native reader is unmounted.

+## C1 review fix round1 — original observation lifetime
+
+Root's pre-code ruling and exact P2 are in [the independent review](../reviews/2026-11-02-finite-pre-t0-qa-provenance.md), committed before this fix. FixBASE6335b5d2410527b5ebf6beb4dc628205cde73045 contains only Root documentation after original DocHEADb7ca96be80da939ad906c52e5b2ab900e901e4cd.
+
+The finding was valid: final issuer primary reconciliation treated original observedAt as a response that must remain younger than5s. Multiple individually valid requests could create/read back the tag/ref within original Pend, then fail the job only because the observation aged. That unnecessarily consumed the write-once publication.
+
+Production change is confined to mcp-qa-finite-bootstrap-primary.mjs issuer historical-observation predicate and explanatory comment. It now requires a safe integer observedAt within original step/Pend and not after the primary observation time. It does NOT impose cumulative age<=5s. The original timestamp remains unchanged. Per-response Date freshness, transport child5s, original admission wall/monotonic clock, P0/Pend, source/job/step checks, spent cap, unknown-ACK behavior, credentials and native reader phase are unchanged. No extension, retry/reset/replacement or owner window.

+## Implemented behavior
+
+The actual host first invokes custody's internal preflight inside its existing error/cleanup route; custody repeats the guard before beginAdmission. Native source is optional all-or-none in createFiniteNativePrimaryReader, opaque and source-owned. It captures actual DB/ctx/storage/namespace/version/program/control provenance. The existing gate privately retains it; no registration, arbitrary callbacks, ready DTO, caller SQL or reservation hash grants permission. Internal custody-scope checks bind the gate to its actual DB and original selectors. Writer lease objects are privately tied to their issuing gate/kind before SQL generation.
+

No #finite/#host/cleanup-factory/nativeCleanup.close recursion was added. Existing source-less historical cleanup constructions stay inert for admission.
+
+The fixed first-primary cardinality-checked query verifies the exact prepared reservation and its actual original admission/closure joins. It requires slot/trial/scopeHash/application/predecessor pins/candidate program/source/container/exact window/budget1/state/closed_at, T0<=reserved_at<=SQL_NOW and start<=SQL_NOW<min(N1,candidate expiry,selector expiry). The actual ordinary admission INSERT...SELECT .run() adds the same source-owned EXISTS; manifest expiry<=reservation expiry and original issued-lease SQL deadline remain inside that EXISTS. Original grant/consent/revocation/scopes/closure/time predicates remain. No global admissionsCOUNT=1 was added; that remains reservation creation's distinct gate. Historical reservation observed_at does not renew a lifetime, and existing launch retirement/deployment fences remain unchanged.
+
+Known-no-admission errors are exact private objects of one custody/current attempt. Only its own host recognizes them; public failures remain sanitized. A fresh definitive pre-marker denial can suppress close only after valid custody/gate reads and a successful source query showing no candidate admission/launch/closure. Any admission/launch writer, row, custody record or ambiguous prior observation disqualifies fresh classification. Root explicitly approved BEFORE product that reservation-only issued/pending markers do not themselves imply possible process: rejecting a premature subscription must not poison legitimate reservation completion. No marker is reset/refunded.
+
+After beginAdmission, the marker remains spent/unresolved even for zero rows. Cumulative pending/executed/unsafe/possible-launch evidence lives outside the retriable transaction callback. Only success===true/meta.changes===0 for every execution and the exact private zero-execution failure can suppress close. changes1, thrown uniqueness, lost ACK, successfalse, malformed metadata, any non-zero-specific callback failure or possible launch stays unsafe. A later zero never erases earlier inserted/unknown/unreadable-custody evidence. Existing settled ready admission may use the original reconcile-only path, never a new admission/launch.
+

Standalone createSuccessorLaunchGate and its existing lease fixtures are unchanged separately owned APIs; journal begin* methods alone are not effect permission. This unit does not establish standalone launch authority or actual Worker finite wiring/factory qualification.

No runtime fix, rollout, main merge, publication, provider/Container/cron/Workflow CREATE, token/role/OAuth change, owner window, Max/mail/chat/automation or real attempt occurred. No local source/clones/dependencies/builds/tempfiles/processes; only bounded skill text reads used local access. STOP/release sole-writer role for Root's fresh independent reviewers.
+
+Evidence links: [final run](https://github.com/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/actions/runs/41537047289), [final job](https://github.com/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/actions/runs/41537047289/job/125591439327), [exact code](https://github.com/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/commit/06e424666e7cc1fcf277024aaed463f7d764dc24).

+# AFW — preparación: esquema público y metadata fechada (2nov2026)
+
+SOURCE/PREFLIGHT ONLY. operationalReadiness=false. No ensayo, OAuth, permiso/token, código remoto, activación, cron, Container o suscripción modificado.
+
+Root ejecutó exactamente4GET mediante Cloudflare MCP, cuenta AFW85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec: listado de deployments per_page1 y metadata por UUID observado, únicamente QA y canary. Sin include=modules, texto de bindings privados, secretos, autores, otros proyectos ni sondeos de negocio. El puntero se observó una vez; no se confirmó estabilidad posterior ni cierre integral actual. Metadata de una versión no prueba ctx.container ni ejecución/retirada física.
+
+Proyección saneada:
+
+```json
+[
+  {
+    "role": "qa",
+    "observedAt": "2026-11-02T12:35:05.100Z",
+    "deploymentId": "4c78c652-5e8a-43e6-a622-bb0def85b9a1",

+        },

+        }
+      },
+      "containers": null,

+          "type": "version_metadata"
+        }
+      ],
+      "rateLimitBindings": [

+        }
+      ],
+      "ownedNamespaceBindings": [

+          "namespace_id": "070c4a7708eb4592a3ded7c117a88225",

+        }
+      ]
+    },

+    "operationalReadiness": false

+    "versionId": "2607abae-cec3-4e35-89eb-8b8572262b54",
+    "metadataByExactVersion": {
+      "exports": {
+        "AfwOwnEventNode": {
+          "type": "durable-object",
+          "storage": "sqlite",
+          "container": "agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary-afwowneventnode"
+        }
+      },
+      "containers": [
+        {
+          "class_name": "AfwOwnEventNode",
+          "name": "agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary-afwowneventnode"
+        }
+      ],
+      "versionMetadataBindings": [],
+      "rateLimitBindings": [
+        {
+          "name": "DELEGATED_RATE_LIMITER",
+          "type": "ratelimit"
+        }
+      ],
+      "ownedNamespaceBindings": [
+        {
+          "name": "AFW_DOSSIER_EVENTS_NODE",
+          "namespace_id": "d6fc71552de7494a863aca20365a572d",
+          "class_name": "AfwOwnEventNode"
+        }
+      ]

+    "metadataCurrentConfirmed": false,
+    "operationalReadiness": false
+  }

+```
+
+Esquema primario consultado: [Create Worker Version](https://developers.cloudflare.com/api/resources/workers/subresources/beta/subresources/workers/subresources/versions/methods/create/). La ruta Beta admite cuerpo JSON y módulos base64; deploy=false separa carga/activación. El descriptor inherit debe fijar version_id; los endpoints clásicos de scripts no admiten UUID explícito en ese campo. Declarative exports puede reconciliar namespaces, por lo que debe preservarse exactamente el conjunto/backend/enlace existente de clases DO: no tombstones, transferencias, nuevas clases/namespaces ni migrations por inferencia. Campos de petición y metadata observada requieren normalización explícita, no copiar todo el resultado del GET. Esto documenta esquema, no permiso/API aceptados en AFW.
+
+Ruling Root para C2T3: transportar JSON real de esta ruta, no reutilizar multipart clásico. Mantener la declaración de Container existente de AfwOwnEventNode en los perfiles y en la fase finite; no publicar el actual marcador source containers[] como si conservara el enlace. Cualquier cambio de plantilla es SOURCE ONLY con tests/dry-run y hashes regenerados; no crea app/imagen/namespace/migración ni acredita capacidad física. El payload de API usa únicamente su gramática admitida, y el fingerprint normalizado conserva los algoritmos existentes. Ante deriva/reconciliación inesperada, pendiente administrativo; no borrar/recrear recursos automáticamente.

## docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-03-finite-initial-activation-coordinator.md

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-03-finite-initial-activation-coordinator.md b/docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-03-finite-initial-activation-coordinator.md
--- /dev/null
+++ b/docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-03-finite-initial-activation-coordinator.md
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+# Coordinador de activación inicial: preparación de fuente
+
+PROJECT AFW; repositorio tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web; rama feat/finite-native-cleanup-20261101. Unidad de código y pruebas `77c93ee83e9f2d9e883a5284280973cffa30f7e6`, árbol `9475bd87bd02f9b5694c70fee412c6158fec964c`. Entorno sintético en GitHub Actions. Ninguna llamada de activación, credencial real, despliegue ni modificación de proveedor se ejecutó para esta unidad.
+
+La misma invocación ganadora B1 → A conserva productor, lease, holders y etapa retenida durante la carga inactiva, el armado y las comprobaciones primarias y seriales. El armado devuelve privadamente su plan y ACK reales. Tras los gates se reserva un único ActivationSend por transacción; sync y readback preceden al POST fijo. El ACK deriva de la respuesta real y Completion requiere lectura primaria del deployment y la versión, bytes y configuración exactos; ambos registros se sincronizan y releen. Un journal preexistente o resultado incierto cierra sin reemisión. El RPC conserva salida factual e incompleta.
+
+El fetch de activación y sus cuerpos se retienen hasta liquidación real aun después del vencimiento lógico. No se renuevan el plazo original ni el límite hijo. El RED nativo de timeout en `ead8e22`, Actions `41688119539` job `126099185351`, mostró que abortar el fetch liberaba el scope mientras el handler sintético seguía retenido; `f2b5196` conserva el fetch y el caso pasa en la ejecución final. El RED funcional inicial `abb1833`, Actions `41686578087`, falló específicamente por ausencia de SEND (97/98 focalizadas); consumidores y compilador pasaron.
+
+Actions `41689107277`, sobre la revisión exacta `77c93ee`: 106/106 focalizadas, 83/83 consumidores, 23/23 compilación/residencia, lint acotado aprobado, cero fallos, omisiones o cancelaciones. Incluye POST único, SEND preexistente, fallos antes de SEND sin POST, respuesta perdida o inválida, divergencia tras ACK, sync/retorno de SEND incierto, timeout retenido y reinicio sin reemisión. El runner informó deprecaciones de Node/punycode; no impidieron las pruebas. La compilación midió canary 1.948.550 bytes (SHA256 `185e72f719e0e90b7faf14f2262973c76afbc684c32066a259d1a1de08bb94a7`), QA 1.973.811 bytes (SHA256 `a691142c8eed28be83b4f54296f576a1b7d342a54f50f72cdf4a9206ad2e4b4c`) y paquete comprimido 382.789 bytes (SHA256 `55939471431052eef893ef3195d9eaa2bafe608a5f88f262dcfde19d4e1e4512`). Son medidas de esa revisión, no artefactos desplegados.
+
+La decisión de escritores es el perímetro administrativo confiado de la spec vinculante, no inmutabilidad criptográfica universal. El restorer histórico del mismo destino sería una contradicción si existiera un SEND realmente emitido sin resolver; la revisión histórica indicada en la spec conserva el dispatch anterior incierto y el guard bloqueado que no emitió su SEND. No se inventa una prueba universal ni se declara drenaje físico. Un escritor legítimo contrario, evidencia de reescritura o efecto relevante emitido sin resolver bloquea.
+
+El techo de fuente sigue vencido y no se modifica. La aceptación real requiere además comprobar credenciales y capacidad del endpoint, reconciliar techo y C1, medir latencia, resolver divergencias de configuración primaria, completar cápsula y traspaso de owner/closed UI y cleanup, y revisar la rama. La disponibilidad con PC apagada no está probada. Estos pasos no están autorizados por esta preparación de fuente.
+
+
+## Fix round 1: retención del ciclo completo de respuesta
+
+FIX_BASE `46fd7b7a4534379f826a661d42645039a8483fbc`. El revisor detectó que retener fetch/read/cancel por separado dejaba una ventana entre la liquidación de la última promesa retenida y el inicio de la cancelación del cuerpo. Con el lease cerrado, podía abrirse otro scope mientras la cancelación seguía pendiente. `lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-entry.mjs` retiene ahora la promesa del trabajo HTTP completo y espera en `finally` la liquidación de la cancelación, sin prolongar el plazo lógico ni habilitar reenvíos. El fixture nativo y `test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-native.test.mjs` comprueban headers tardíos y lectura tardía, cancelación retenida, ausencia de ACK tardío y POST único.
+
+RED `4cd75ba0925f98d60d84b43c0e3c3e0381624e35`, Actions `41690504588` job `126107508662`: 106 PASS, una falla esperada porque el scope se abrió con la cancelación pendiente. GREEN `bd460633e3a8b39cad9cc5b63555aed034678013`, árbol `c3ce148fd20f2148630d41a2b24941bf674bb4df`, Actions `41691101695`: 108 PASS focalizadas, 83 PASS consumidores, 23 PASS compilación/residencia, cero fallos/cancelaciones/omisiones y lint acotado aprobado. Se ejecutaron los comandos existentes de `.github/workflows/finite-task1-temporary-qualifier.yml`: `node --test test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-workflows-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-history-schema-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-old-guard-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-gates-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-custody-probe-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-scope.test.mjs`; `node --test --test-concurrency=1 test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-entry-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-native.test.mjs`; `node --test test/mcp-qa-finite-candidate-residency.test.mjs`; y el lint acotado declarado en ese workflow. Node/punycode emitieron advertencias de deprecación.
+
+El compilador midió canary 1.948.550 bytes SHA256 `eda5ef06e94b83cec6d287d08f505b89fe80ead8de8622c24ae0978e63dc45c4` y QA 1.973.973 bytes SHA256 `3ece3728e9a641743bf59d4f67b5d3221f7942f2fb722667bb21c34b9105f792`, únicamente para esa revisión de fuente. Sin cambios de credenciales, techo ni proveedor real. Esta corrección queda pendiente de revisión por el mismo revisor.

+# Datos de activación inicial, sin montar
+
+Base 6f40c14dfb241d84f3f8565542dd3591f849494d. Código 63e159ed77d8b4eb0e0edae8337f377317c9aa18, pruebas ampliadas e391789783e8c93aab94ff942f95e0a2775d59d7, rama feat/finite-native-cleanup-20261101.
+
+La pieza pura construye el body fijo y SEND propuesto con la versión candidata y la anotación vinculada al plan. Verifica la correlación de entrada, plan, estado inicial y arm-ack mediante los codecs existentes. Las transformaciones posteriores comprueban la forma del resultado de deployment, enlaces y límites temporales de ACK y completion, la publicación observada y los bytes/configuración de la candidata con el calificador existente.
+
+Estas funciones devuelven datos sin efectos ni autoridad. Los tiempos y respuestas son entradas del llamador y no prueban procedencia, custodia, reloj real, persistencia, observación ni permiso. Ninguna ruta, actor o productor de producción las importa. Los timestamps de la prueba son sintéticos derivados del armado del fixture, no resultados de Cloudflare. El módulo no realiza GET, POST ni transacciones.
+
+La prueba test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire-native.test.mjs usa un método exclusivo del actor sintético que lee la entrada y armado reales del fixture, genera datos sintéticos de provider y comprueba que no cambian los registros ni las solicitudes. Verifica la ruta, body, vínculos, y rechazos por arm alterado, versión, anotación, deployment o módulo incorrecto, SEND anterior al armado, límite superior a cinco segundos, ACK y observación fuera de plazo.
+
+El primer intento 6592f9753d682f0dd6bd320ca180281a29ea3e1e no incluía el módulo y produjo un fallo de construcción de fixture, no un RED conductual. El stub cerrado 7f2da2658f8ac11bf3a69dcf5f0e293ed3639574 produjo el RED aislado en Actions 41655522106: 76 PASS y 1 FAIL esperado en propuesta ausente. La implementación 63e159ed pasó Actions 41656117780. Las cuatro comprobaciones temporales añadidas en e391789783e8c93aab94ff942f95e0a2775d59d7 pasaron Actions 41656730123.
+
+El comando focal de CI añade `node --test test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-workflows-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-history-schema-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-old-guard-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-gates-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-scope.test.mjs`. Resultado 81 PASS, 0 FAIL, 0 SKIP. Consumidores 83 PASS, compilación/residencia 23 PASS, lint aprobado, cero artefactos.
+
+Pendiente para un emisor real: calificar los escritores relevantes e inmutabilidad histórica de manera suficiente, la capacidad/validez de la credencial nativa y el presupuesto real. Conservar la autoridad del B1/A vivo y los holders. Tras la última comprobación serial, transacción SEND única y sync/readback bajo el hijo original, un solo POST fijo, ACK de respuesta real, lectura primaria de deployment/versión/módulo/configuración y Completion durable. SEND existente o resultado incierto nunca reemite. La cápsula, entrada autenticada, traspaso y ensayo end-to-end siguen pendientes. Esta unidad no prueba PC-off.
+
+## Fix round 1: módulo vinculado al plan
+
+Base de revisión b2f221f415f1b98ee3e3a6157a5ea87daab62678. La revisión detectó que la calificación aceptaba bytes y metadata equivocados pero coherentes entre sí, sin compararlos con el módulo del plan armado.
+
+RED 1431a34b6cda259e7ad17f255f25c9c2ba78ebc9, Actions 41657701660: 81 PASS y 1 FAIL causal en `activation wire rejects coherent-wrong-module`. La fixture sintética sustituyó bytes y metadata de forma coherente, y el código anterior los aceptó. El fix a13ba335d82154fb6b563698e31a90e9d304b228 exige Uint8Array, tamaño y SHA256 iguales a moduleBytes y moduleHash del plan antes de calificar la metadata. La prueba ampliada fbdfd018e6856ccf3076d215bb95c9aeede0440e también modifica un byte conservando el mismo tamaño y actualiza la metadata, para ejercitar el hash por separado.
+
+Actions 41658302489 sobre fbdfd018 ejecutó el comando focal documentado arriba, con 83 PASS, 0 FAIL, 0 SKIP. Grupo de consumidores 83 PASS, compilación/residencia 23 PASS, lint acotado aprobado, cero artefactos. No se agregó red, almacenamiento, emisor o autoridad.

~~~~~~~~~~

+# Immutable initial activation capsule — implementation qualification 4 November 2026
+
+Status: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED AND CI GREEN, pending independent task review. operationalReadiness=false;completePackage=false;PC-off pending. Source branch feat/finite-native-cleanup-20261101. Full task BASE006bf8578ad27fd7f20abc00a9418f369b2a8b4c; exact code/test HEADbae656253793eca490e9d7d38cd3a8cb142ba0c4, tree222b65054853154110c2f37ddf5fd7e4fc3db910. This report is controller bookkeeping from actual GitHub receipts after the implementation agent's final response was interrupted by model capacity; its partial self-review is not an independent review.
+
+The unit adds strict capsule codecs/link qualification, original A private material-store witnesses and a fixed private capsule append in the existing activation continuation after actual Completion sync/readback. Captured original parent/stage/holders and no-effect-authority constraints are preserved by the implementation claim to be verified in fresh review. An early orphan/preexisting capsule fence prevents beginning another Entry/effect; atomic append also requires absent capsule and finite HEAD. Actual M UUID/deployment and A identity are recorded, old2607 remains rollback. bindingsHash follows the explicit complete sorted canonical bindings SHA convention, distinct from the public projection. Existing cleanup codecs/inventory, safe RPC shape, expired ceiling and unavailable owner seams remain unchanged.
+
+TDD sourcecd5fa42c3a6e56f5bd4cf2750dd7de5cb46770e7, Actions41694127911/focused126120046559:107PASS1FAIL exact completed activation must persist immutable capsule. Compiler and forward consumers passed. Root read actual overnight logs and confirmed the causal RED; it was not rerun unchanged.
+
+First implementation229ae5d74461c3327d7c9da264d8db9f33a1c384, Actions41871990378: compiler passed, consumers exposed old11sync expectation after added capsule sync, and focused job reached its7minute bound/CANCELLED. Agent reported the held-transaction-read fixture attempted release over a native actor input gate that its own transaction held; Miniflare Promise parser rejection and stranded fixture work followed. It is not treated as a product or provider failure nor as causal RED. Fixbae6562 uses after-native-commit transaction-return hold, retains sync/readback and Completion scenarios, adds early orphan fence and updates B1 assertion to require capsule and12syncs. Newly touched RPC results dispose in finally; preexisting disposal warnings are not all attributed to the new test.
+
+Exact GREEN Actions41873478253, attempt1, headbae6562, conclusionSUCCESS independently read by root:
+- focused job126726553638:140PASS0FAIL0CANCELLED0SKIPPED; scoped lintPASS.
+- forward-consumers126726553130:102PASS0FAIL0CANCELLED0SKIPPED.
+- compiler126726553927:23PASS0FAIL0CANCELLED0SKIPPED.
+Root downloaded these source-only job logs and extracted actual summaries. Compiler log transient MCP read error succeeded on one subsequent read; no tests were repeated to obtain logs.
+
+Commands at exact source .github/workflows/finite-task1-temporary-qualifier.yml:
+focused node --test includes initial-cleanup-native/records, initial-capsule-native/records, initial-workflows-native, initial-history-schema-native, initial-old-guard-native, initial-gates-native, initial-activation-wire-native, initial-custody-probe-native and initial-scope.
+consumers node --test --test-concurrency=1 test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-entry-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.test.mjs.
+compiler node --test test/mcp-qa-finite-candidate-residency.test.mjs.

+# Lecturas previas de la activación inicial
+
+Alcance de código en la rama finite. Estas lecturas producen hechos acotados. Ninguna concede autoridad para activar, reemplazar ni restaurar.
+
+## Inventario de Workflows
+
+Código c05b13059901b3bf518fa81b7499850eeb3923b0, desde BASE 6343174247655479196e2e1d32968bcdc3b06c39. readFiniteInitialAfwWorkflows valida el registro de entrada, conserva su plazo y usa el enumerador fijo existente de las tres definiciones. Admite una sola instancia inicial exacta en bootstrap, en running o waiting. Exige que las demás estén terminadas. Devuelve solo conteos y hechos, sin capacidad de efecto.
+
+RED ba5b399164cd82c0e28a0f374f90e6fe96a880a7, run 41635614620, job 125915915866, 45 PASS y una falla esperada por gate ausente. GREEN c05b130, run 41636097331, 46 focalizadas, 83 consumidores y 23 compilación/residencia, lint. Prueba nativa cubre instancia propia ausente, terminada, duplicada, ID incorrecto, instancia ajena y otra definición activa. La ampliación 1a5b51d8bc6fb73211870a767e0a938826d3f22a agrega waiting válido y comprobaciones del lease del esquema.
+
+## Esquema instalado del historial
+
+Código df9b29c9b4c9867619d47f28f38457af5f5d2104. readFiniteInitialHistorySchema consulta con first-primary las cuatro definiciones concretas de triggers BEFORE UPDATE/DELETE para admissions y closures. Comprueba nombres, tabla y SQL fijado, utiliza el lease original y limita la lectura a cinco segundos. El resultado afirma la observación actual del esquema. No acredita que siempre haya estado instalado ni impide inferir que SQLite REPLACE respeta los triggers sin evidencia adicional.
+
+RED b634b4b04bbafe0ccdd29b5861fda9983f3e81dd, run 41636714974, job 125919642856, 46 PASS y una falla esperada por lector ausente. GREEN 1a5b51d8, run 41637730238, 47 focalizadas, 83 consumidores, 23 compilación/residencia, lint, cero artefactos. En D1 sintético se quitó y alteró cada trigger y luego se restauró. Los parent abortados o vencidos fueron rechazados.
+
+## Guard histórico fijo
+
+Código 4a3e720df343c99f5969cdf817de44fb28e4b8b6. readFiniteInitialOldGuard consulta exclusivamente la instancia histórica fijada por la ruta del Workflow, con el lector nativo y GET acotado a 256 KiB y cinco segundos. El límite es una cota de implementación, no una medida observada del cuerpo real. Valida complete, seis pasos, la salida blocked/dispatch uncertain/baja no verificada y el paso de restauración exitoso con sus tiempos históricos. La respuesta conserva historicalDispatch uncertain y no afirma drenaje universal. Se exige el lease original. No se exige result.success ni result.error porque su valor no se verificó.
+
+RED 047f1abe7eee2659c74cb1fbb54843d257e99c30, run 41638355349, job 125925180962, 47 PASS y una falla esperada por lector ausente. GREEN 4a3e720, run 41638779104, 48 focalizadas, 83 consumidores y 23 compilación/residencia, cero fallos/omisiones/cancelaciones, lint y cero artefactos. La prueba nativa alteró estado, salida, paso, fechas, cantidad, HTTP y lease.
+
+La observación real primaria del guard realizada por dirección el 3 de noviembre a las 12:41:55 UTC se hizo con la sesión MCP, sobre el recurso fijado. La capacidad del token nativo para ese endpoint sigue sin comprobarse. El source histórico 2a477 usa INSERT para admissions e INSERT OR IGNORE para closures, como evidencia limitada a esos escritores. Permanece pendiente calificar otros posibles escritores y el efecto de REPLACE en D1 antes de usar la ausencia histórica como conclusión.
+
+## Pendiente de composición
+
+Los tres lectores no están conectados al productor. Queda verificarlos bajo el mismo B1/A, el presupuesto compartido y sus holders, cualificar custodia y puntero actual, emitir SEND una sola vez, comprobar ACK/publicación y crear la cápsula. El techo y la custodia anteriores ya vencieron. Esta unidad no emitió ninguna llamada real de activación ni acredita entrega, cierre físico o prueba con PC apagado.

+# Composición preliminar de hechos en la entrada inicial
+
+BASE ef5b9686c39caf543254214d68485850a4057d7c. Código 9704e724d8678919cbfff8af5724980e5cc7b949, pruebas ampliadas 8cbac3dae741aadf4211df02784f91ab8c227a66. La entrada sigue devolviendo material_recorded solo cuando sus comprobaciones terminan. Un fallo adicional devuelve admin_pending y conserva la alarma y la carga inactiva ya asentadas.
+
+Después de la carga inactiva y el armado real, la misma invocación B1/A comprueba plan, estado, ACK, alarma y ausencia de HEAD. En paralelo, con el mismo lease original y sus holders, consulta el esquema instalado, el guard histórico exacto, el inventario de Workflows y nuevamente el historial primario. La historia nueva debe coincidir con los hashes del material A. También compara el puntero QA con la prueba bootstrap obtenida por A y verifica identidad, estado y vencimiento de ambos tokens. Repite puntero, custodia y la lectura de armado antes de devolver. Las lecturas conservan límites hijos y el plazo original; el resultado transportado sigue siendo factual y no concede efectos. Ante error o vencimiento se cierra el padre, sin emitir activación.
+
+RED 05445b0240017a6aaca3374dff6a88ffd416b6b3, run 41639900002, job 125930461668, 48 PASS y cuatro fallas esperadas por composición ausente. GREEN 9704e724, run 41640406620, 52 focalizadas, 83 consumidores y 23 compilación/residencia, lint. Ampliación de pruebas 8cbac3da, run 41640941700, 55 focalizadas, 83 consumidores y 23 compilación/residencia, cero fallos/cancelaciones/omisiones, lint y cero artefactos. En el actor workerd/SQLite y D1 sintético se comprobaron el paso positivo y rechazos tras el armado por guard, inventario, trigger, QA, historial y custodia alterados. No hubo POST de activación ni SEND. Son respuestas sintéticas y no acreditan capacidad remota de la credencial.
+
+Para la revisión 8cbac3da el compilador midió canary 1948550 bytes, SHA256 0a5b9a0d7545151b8a38f5f673b0f2ca5da6942be54996ba8cd2fb01c94a75b1 y QA 1955383 bytes, SHA256 23cbe89407cbd5934586ebd7ad6de66acc10784263eca9a9a41bd41ff038531c. El comprimido fue 382787 bytes, SHA256 90cb9ce262d7fc88aa2fccddb45c0f09bf9b721427710a5bbf9e8395f14d329c. Son medidas de esa revisión.
+
+La composición es preliminar. Falta verificar todos los escritores relevantes, el aislamiento histórico incluyendo REPLACE, la capacidad real del lector nativo para Workflows, el estado de Container, schedules y tails, la elegibilidad actual de reversión, el puntero canary y el último control previo a SEND. Falta implementar el emisor único y su ACK, el readback primario y la cápsula. La lectura de esquema afirma solo el estado instalado observado, no la inmutabilidad histórica universal. QA bootstrap conserva su hipótesis de confianza del escritor del repositorio. La custodia y el techo fijados están vencidos. No se ejecutó un ensayo remoto ni PC-off.
+
+## Fix round 1: retención del trabajo real pendiente
+
+El revisor detectó que los lectores concurrentes posteriores a A se ejecutaban fuera del ámbito AsyncLocalStorage de A. Si una rama de Promise.all fallaba, su promesa agregada podía resolverse antes de que una consulta D1 o lectura de cuerpo de otra rama terminara, dejando de retenerse el trabajo nativo.
+
+Código 3ed0866273eb7e2ed38a9cf1b79da727f93f2b52 reingresa en el ámbito ya existente de A mediante continueFiniteOwnerStageWork. Exige el mismo ctx, env, storage, D1 y lease original activo, conserva la admisión interna existente y no crea una capacidad ni extiende plazos. El callback fuente abarca toda la composición y las operaciones de lectores anidados quedan en el mismo registro hasta su liquidación. La salida ante fallo sigue siendo acotada.
+
+El RED causal e60d52ea8df47c59d47269e0b1805078cd505317, run 41642534900, job 125939405946, tuvo 55 PASS y una falla: con D1 nativo retenido y el guard rechazado, un segundo ámbito podía abrirse antes de terminar el D1. Se comprobó que el D1 estaba efectivamente retenido y no se registró SEND. Dos intentos previos del fixture e6500eb5 y 63d4f83b fallaron al compilar por secuencias de escape incorrectas del fixture, corregidas en e60d52e; no son evidencia de la regresión.
+
+En GREEN 6492ada61884f3ff3d7975183efc15f3a1d5d921, run 41643214018, el comando `node --test test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-workflows-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-history-schema-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-old-guard-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-gates-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-scope.test.mjs` registró 57 PASS, cero fallos/omisiones/cancelaciones, con lint acotado. El caso nativo mantuvo bloqueado el ámbito hasta liberar el D1 y comprobó journal sin SEND ni escritura tardía. La prueba de ámbito rechazó otro contexto, otro lease, cambio de storage o D1 y el lease abortado. El grupo de consumidores registró 83 PASS y el de compilación/residencia 23 PASS; cero artefactos. Todas son pruebas sintéticas en CI y no acreditan un ensayo remoto.
